# Skills · M · part 110

825 entries, multi-persona-review to multi-tier-audit-pipeline. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **multi-persona-review**: Conducts iterative multi-expert simulated audits of complex web applications using specialized reviewer personas. Use this skill when auditing features from multiple perspectives (3D model/architect, desktop UX, mobile UX, domain specialist/lighting designer), generating round-based QA reports, and prioritizing fixes into actionable checklists. ([Polifem88/fest-stage](https://github.com/Polifem88/fest-stage/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/multi-persona-review))
- **multi-persona-review-panel**: Convene a small panel of independent reviewer lenses on a diff before opening a PR. Each lens reads the change against the repository as it is, not as the PR description says it is, and returns findings with file and line plus one verdict. Use before opening a PR for anything beyond a trivial edit. Complements a correctness-focused code-review pass: this skill covers the lenses a pure bug hunt misses (architecture, security, test and quality coverage, performance). ([FortunaTerra-Group/multi-persona-review-panel-skill](https://github.com/FortunaTerra-Group/multi-persona-review-panel-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/multi-persona-review-panel))
- **multi-perspective**: Explore a research question through several independent analytical perspectives and synthesize their agreements and disagreements. Use when one line of reasoning is insufficient and distinct viewpoints should be preserved. ([flonat/flonat-research](https://github.com/flonat/flonat-research/tree/HEAD/skills/multi-perspective) · ★153)
- **multi-perspective**: Execute the same request with 5 different specialized agents in parallel, then synthesize results with a reviewer agent to produce the best possible outcome. Triggers on phrases like "multiple perspectives", "5 agents", "ensemble analysis", "compare approaches", or "/multi-perspective".
